# MEDULLA-CQ-CONN requirements

| ID | Requirement | Verification |
|---|---|---|
| `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R001` | **ansible-tower competency rollup.** Subsystem: competency, entry served `Method::Uql`. The ontology answers one single-source and one cross-source question over the `ansible-tower` connector's access contract. The contract is fetched at query time, with no copy of the source. Children `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R001.1` and `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R001.2` hold the questions as entries of the table in MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.9. Acceptance is the one table-driven served test; there is no per-connector acceptance row. | Test: `tests/connector_competency/test_competency_served.py::test_case` (L3) passes for entries `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R001.1` and `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R001.2`. |
| `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R001.1` | **ansible-tower single-source question.** Subsystem: competency, entry served `Method::Uql`. Caller: the table entry `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R001.1` of MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.9 → `tests/connector_competency/test_competency_served.py::test_case` → `Method::Uql`. Question: `Which job templates failed in their last run, and against which inventory hosts?` Sources: `ansible-tower-mcp`; access contracts from CONN-ANSIBLE-TOWER-MCP-CONNECTOR-CONTENT-R002. Rule: For each template take the run with the latest `finishedAt`. Keep it when `jobStatus` is `failed`. Answer columns: templateName, hostId, finishedAt. Vocabulary: `infrastructure-v1.ttl`; producer terms: `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R068.2` (AutomationJob), `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R068.3`, `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R068.4` (jobStatus), `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R068.5` (ranAgainstHost). Every answer row carries source, record id and as-of. Abstention: an unconnected source yields `NotUsedReason::Unconnected` naming it. Writes: none. Depends on: MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.9, MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R722.5, CONN-ANSIBLE-TOWER-MCP-CONNECTOR-CONTENT-R002. | Test: `tests/connector_competency/test_competency_served.py::test_case` (L3), entry `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R001.1`. It asserts the non-empty provenance-carrying answer on the recorded data and the typed abstention when one source is unregistered. |
| `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R001.2` | **ansible-tower cross-source question.** Subsystem: competency, entry served `Method::Uql`. Caller: the table entry `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R001.2` of MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.9 → `tests/connector_competency/test_competency_served.py::test_case` → `Method::Uql`. Question: `Which hosts that failed their last Tower run also have an open ServiceNow change or incident?` Sources: `ansible-tower-mcp`, `servicenow-api`, `systems-manager`; access contracts from CONN-ANSIBLE-TOWER-MCP-CONNECTOR-CONTENT-R002, CONN-SERVICENOW-API-CONNECTOR-CONTENT-R008, CONN-SYSTEMS-MANAGER-CONNECTOR-CONTENT-R002. Rule: Join `job_run.hostId`, `incident.cmdbCi` and `host.host_id` on the host id. Keep failed last runs and incidents whose `state` is not closed. Answer columns: hostId, hostname, number. Vocabulary: `infrastructure-v1.ttl`; producer terms: `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R068.2` to `R068.5`. Every answer row carries source, record id and as-of. Abstention: an unconnected source yields `NotUsedReason::Unconnected` naming it. Writes: none. Depends on: MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.9, MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R722.5, CONN-ANSIBLE-TOWER-MCP-CONNECTOR-CONTENT-R002, CONN-SERVICENOW-API-CONNECTOR-CONTENT-R008, CONN-SYSTEMS-MANAGER-CONNECTOR-CONTENT-R002. | Test: `tests/connector_competency/test_competency_served.py::test_case` (L3), entry `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R001.2`. | `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R045.1` | **owncast single-source question.** Question: `Which streams had the highest peak viewer count and how long did they run?` The owncast connector alone answers it, carrying provenance (source, record id, as-of). Vocabulary: `viewerCount` on `:BroadcastStream` in `media-v1.ttl`. The case does not use `peakViewerCount`, whose domain is `:LiveStream`. | A fixture query over owncast data returns a non-empty provenance-carrying answer, or a typed abstention naming the unconnected source. |
| `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R045.2` | **owncast cross-source question.** Question: `Which Owncast broadcasts were announced through a Postiz post, and what was the audience versus the post reach?` Stage: competency over the `owncast` connector, entry served `Uql`. Caller: served `ConnectorPack.Import` (MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R722.3) registers the sources, then the served `Uql` method reaches `crates/medulla-plan/src/federation_opt/run.rs::foreign_scan`. Code: case `q2` in `tests/connector_competency/cases/owncast.json` run by `tests/connector_competency/test_competency_served.py::test_case`. Vocabulary: `crates/medulla-core/ontology/media-v1.ttl` and `content-v1.ttl`. Graph pattern: `?s a :BroadcastStream ; :viewerCount ?audience`; `?p a :SocialPost ; :announcesStream ?s`; `?m a :EngagementMetric ; :metricOf ?p ; :engagementMetricName "reach" ; :engagementMetricValue ?reach`. The case lists the cross-source set: owncast and postiz. Mattermost is dropped from the source list: no mattermost term links a message to a stream. Rule: the source is read at query time and no source row is stored. Each answer row carries connector, contract IRI, mapping IRI and fetch time (MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R722.5). The answer columns are stream, audience, post, reach. A wrong answer is a failure, not an abstention. | `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R052.1` | **repository-manager single-source question.** Question: `Which worktrees are stale or dangling relative to their base branch?` Stage: competency over the `repository-manager` connector, entry served `Uql`. Caller: served `ConnectorPack.Import` (MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R722.3) registers the sources, then the served `Uql` method reaches `crates/medulla-plan/src/federation_opt/run.rs::foreign_scan`. Code: case `q1` in `tests/connector_competency/cases/repository-manager.json` run by `tests/connector_competency/test_competency_served.py::test_case`. Vocabulary: `crates/medulla-core/ontology/sdlc_lifecycle-v1.ttl`. Graph pattern: `?w a :Worktree ; :worktreeState ?s ; :tracksBranch ?b`; `FILTER(?s IN ("stale", "dangling"))`. The case lists the single-source set: repository-manager. Rule: the source is read at query time and no source row is stored. Each answer row carries connector, contract IRI, mapping IRI and fetch time (MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R722.5). The answer columns are worktree, state, branch. A wrong answer is a failure, not an abstention. Refusals: `Unconnected` names the missing source (MEDULLA-SRC-AUTH-R006.2). `SOURCE_HELD` names a held mapping. `SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE` flags a down source (MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R722.4). Evidence: the `AuthorityAudit` of MEDULLA-SRC-AUTH-R007.1 lists each source used and its reason. Writes: none; `Uql` is read-only. Depends on: MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.9, MEDULLA-SRC-AUTH-R007.1. | Test: `tests/connector_competency/test_competency_served.py::test_case[repository-manager.q1]` (L3) asserts the columns, a non-empty answer, the provenance fields and zero stored source rows. `test_case_abstains[repository-manager.q1]` (L3) asserts `Unconnected` naming each source when its mapping is absent. |

Caller: the test → `foreign_scan` in `crates/medulla-plan/src/federation_opt/run.rs`. Rule: a source without `vg:materialisation` is `virtual` and stores no row. A source with `vg:materialisation accelerated`, a `vg:materialisationReason`, a `vg:freshnessMaxAgeSeconds` and the connection capability `copy` may keep a watermark-bound cache. The cache is never the source of record. Refusals: the shape of MEDULLA-SRC-AUTH-R002.5 rejects an accelerated row without its reason, freshness bound or `copy` capability. Writes: only the declared cache. Depends on: MEDULLA-SRC-AUTH-R001.5, MEDULLA-SRC-AUTH-R002.5, MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.8. | Test: `tests/connector_competency/test_contract_kinds_served.py::test_6_materialisation` (L3) asserts no stored row without the statement and a watermark-bound cache with it. |

Code: `tests/connector_competency/cases.py::load_cases` reads `tests/connector_competency/cases/<connector>.json`. Each file holds `connector`, `rollup` and a `questions` list. Each file also holds `vocabulary_module` and `terms`, the term IRIs its questions use. Each question holds `id`, `kind` (`single` or `cross`), `uql`, `sources`, `optional_sources`, `columns`, `min_rows` and `recordings`. | `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R085.4` | **Refusal: orphaned ontology and held source abstain by name.** Stage: ontology and query. Entry: served `ConnectorPack.Import`. Caller: `src/server/handlers/admin/connector_pack/import/validation.rs::orphan_policy` and `flag_ontology_orphans`. Rule: with `MEDULLA_ONTOLOGY_ORPHANS=refuse` and an orphaned ontology, import refuses with `ONTOLOGY_ORPHAN` and the question abstains. With the mapping held (not approved), the live part abstains and names `SOURCE_HELD`. A wrong answer is a failure, not an abstention. No standalone admission exists after `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R105`, so the `ONTOLOGY_ORPHAN` refusal stays. Config: `MEDULLA_ONTOLOGY_ORPHANS`, default `warn`, values `warn` or `refuse`. Records written: the refusal in the import record. Depends on: `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R105.1`, `MEDULLA-SRC-AUTH-R006.1` (`SOURCE_HELD` is the `UnapprovedMapping` abstention reason, reported under that code by this question). | L2: `crates/medulla-core/tests/connector_auto_onboard.rs::orphan_and_held_variants_abstain_by_name`. L4: the orphan and held steps of `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R085.6`. |

| `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R089` | **Served vocabulary coverage test for the modules of R071 to R081.** Stage: ontology and packs, then query. Entry: served `ConnectorPack.Import`. Caller: `ConnectorPack.Import` → `src/server/connector_pack_projection.rs::project` → `SourceIngest` (`src/server/handlers/source_ingestion.rs`) → served SPARQL `src/server/handlers/rdf/sparql.rs::handle_sparql`. Code: one table-driven test with one case per term of R071 to R081, keyed by the term name. Each case imports a fixture pack whose ontology class extends the term's class. It ingests one recorded record of that class. It reads the record back with a SPARQL query on the term. It checks source, record id and as-of in the row. Shape cases also send a violating record through served `ShaclValidate` (`src/server/handlers/rdf/validation.rs::handle_shacl_validate`) and expect the shape message. Commercial cases resolve the `MEDULLA-SUPPLY-VOCAB-R003.7` to `R003.12` IRIs for erpnext, salesforce and twenty through registered modules. Refusal: a pack class without the term is flagged `ONTOLOGY_ORPHAN` (`MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R105.1`). A term the live record lacks gives a typed abstention. Records written: the imported pack and the ingested records only. Depends on: `MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R105.1`, `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R071` to `MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R081`. | L4: `tests/typed_packs/test_connector_vocabulary_served.py::test_pack_record_of_each_new_term_round_trips[<term>]` passes for every term case against a served engine. |
